What the record shows

Every conviction, cops and babus alike

682 verified convictions — 682 police officers and 0 civil servants. Every record passed the shared evidence gate: an explicit conviction by a named court with a case reference and an independent verification decision. Records that fail it are withheld, not published.

The Supreme Court maintained the convictions of Reserve Inspector Aftab Ahmad Khan, who with a police party seized two villagers at Rajole on 13 September 1948, shot dead one person and injured others at Korivi, confined the two villagers overnight, and released them on 14 September only after extorting Rs 200.
